一则 Google News RSS 消息援引 finance.biggo.com 报道，称 CoreWeave 正考虑用衍生品对冲内存芯片价格暴跌的风险。

Why it matters:

If a major GPU-cloud buyer is bracing for a price plunge, that's a read-through that the memory-supply tightness propping up AI hardware margins may be cracking.

影响:
CoreWeave 考虑对冲内存成本，说明其预期 DRAM/NAND/HBM 价格将继续下跌——这与支撑 AI 基建资本开支叙事的“内存短缺”说法相悖，更像是供过于求的信号。作为 GPU 云服务的主要买家之一，如果 CoreWeave 都在为价格暴跌做准备，可能意味着撑起 AI 硬件利润率的内存供应紧张正在松动。

Due to shortages of DRAM and NAND, global smartphone shipments are projected to fall 11% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, the lowest level since 2013. - GIGAZINE

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMimgFBVV95cUxNTk5VWWRIR2ZUWHZnTnhWcTVsbk1wVFBQb0dpcVFLYzN2Z2hmX3pCQXpmaWsxeFNTZ09peG9SYUotcGRLek5wRVdKQmhmanNGZDNwcF9lWUllZjZrMG12RktBNC1WeTl5Vm1ZdDdKcGpyd1VIWmpzS0J4OHR2RlJDczZqajZzdkZwYXRaN21TUG9oOVN6enE3QU9R?oc=5" target="_blank">Due to shortages of DRAM and NAND, global smartphone shipments are projected to fall 11% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, the lowest level since 2013.</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color="#6f6f6f">GIGAZINE</font>

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
A GIGAZINE report says DRAM and NAND shortages are cutting global smartphone shipments 11% year-on-year in Q2 2026, the lowest level since 2013.

内容:
GIGAZINE 报道称，受 DRAM 和 NAND 短缺影响，2026 年第二季度全球智能手机出货量预计同比下降 11%，为 2013 年以来最低水平。

Why it matters:
Memory scarcity is now constraining downstream device volumes, not just pricing — a sign the AI-driven HBM/DRAM buildout is crowding out consumer-grade supply. That squeeze pressures margins across the memory supply chain and is a tell that AI capex is distorting adjacent markets.

影响:
内存短缺已经开始压制下游终端设备的出货量，而不只是推高价格——这说明 AI 驱动的 HBM/DRAM 扩产正在挤占消费级内存的供给。这种挤压会压低整个存储产业链的利润率，也是 AI capex 扭曲相邻市场的一个信号。

内容:

Why it matters:
Memory (DRAM/NAND/HBM) is a bottleneck input for AI servers; a supply crunch pushing retail phone prices up signals demand is outstripping fab capacity broadly, not just for HBM. That tightness raises hyperscaler bill-of-materials costs and can squeeze AI-capex margins if it persists.

影响:
内存（DRAM/NAND/HBM）是 AI 服务器的关键瓶颈原材料，连手机零售价都被上调，说明供给紧张已经蔓延到整个存储链条，不只是 HBM。这会推高超大规模厂商的物料成本，若持续下去将挤压 AI capex 的利润空间。

What this means:
Goldman Sachs raised its price target on Applied Materials to $645, citing strong DRAM demand.

内容:
高盛因 DRAM 需求强劲，将应用材料 (Applied Materials) 的目标价上调至 $645。

Why it matters:

内容:

Why it matters:
